MY FILES AND WHAT IS IN THEM (use this order):
1. image_scene1 (Rebecca walking with two kettlebells): use as the opening shot, 3 seconds, slow zoom-in. No voice here, just music + the hook text.
2. video_5: starts with an older woman struggling to get up from a sofa while her daughter helps (about 0-4.5s), then Rebecca on her veranda doing chair squats and talking to the camera (about 4.5-15s). Use all of it, trim silent bits.
3. video_6: Rebecca doing chair squats again (8s). This repeats video_5, so only use 2-3 seconds of it as a quick extra cut if it helps the pace, otherwise skip it.
4. video_7: Rebecca loading crates of mangoes into her pickup and talking (about 0-6s), then sitting on her veranda with a coffee cup (about 6-15s). Use all of it.
5. video_9: this is an exact duplicate of video_7. Do NOT use it.
6. video_8: Rebecca sitting on her veranda with coffee and green hills behind her, saying the final lesson (9s). Use it as the ending.
7. image_scene3 (the sofa scene): only use it if you need a still for the "problem" part; video_5 already shows it.
If any file is missing, edit with what you have and tell me what is missing.

FORMAT
- Vertical 9:16, 1080x1920, 30 fps, MP4 (H.264 + AAC). Final length about 35-45 seconds.
- All clips are horizontal: crop each one to vertical and keep Rebecca's face (or the speaking character's face) inside the frame. Move the crop if she moves.
- Cut silences and dead frames at the start/end of each clip. Keep the pace fast: no shot longer than about 5 seconds without a cut or zoom.
- Hide the small AI sparkle watermark in the bottom-right corner (crop it out).

SUBTITLES (Portuguese)
- Big, bold, ALL CAPS, 2-3 words at a time, white with a thick black outline and a soft shadow, in the lower-middle of the screen (about 30% from the bottom, above the Instagram/TikTok buttons). Each group pops in with a quick scale-up.
- Keywords in RED (#E5202B): 64 ANOS, NINGUÉM, INDEPENDÊNCIA, IDADE, FORÇA, LEVANTAR, CADEIRA, ESCADAS, TODOS OS DIAS, DEZ VEZES, GINÁSIO, VAIDADE, LIBERDADE, HÁBITO.
- The subtitles must match exactly what Rebecca says in each clip. The script she was given is below; if what you hear is different, follow the audio. If you cannot hear the audio, use the script lines in order, place each one over the parts of the clip where there is voice (use silence detection), and tell me so I can check.
  "Tenho 64 anos... e ninguém carrega isto por mim."
  "Sabes o que mais tira a independência a uma mulher depois dos 60? Não é a idade."
  "É perder força. Levantar de uma cadeira fica difícil. Subir escadas também."
  "Por isso, todos os dias: sento e levanto da cadeira. Dez vezes."
  "Levanto caixas da minha colheita, trato da horta, subo a colina. O meu ginásio é a quinta."
  "Força não é vaidade, minha querida. É liberdade. Segue para o hábito número dois."

TEXT CARDS (bold, pop-in animation)
- First 3 seconds, at the top in a black box: "64 ANOS. NINGUÉM CARREGA ISTO POR MIM" ("64 ANOS" in red).
- On the sofa scene, centered: "O MÚSCULO VAI-SE SE NÃO O USARES" ("MÚSCULO" in red).
- On the squats: "10x POR DIA" (big, "10x" in red), with a small counter 1...10 if it fits the timing.
- On the mango crates: "O MEU GINÁSIO = A QUINTA".
- Last 3 seconds, at the top in a black box: "HÁBITO Nº1 · FORÇA" and smaller "Segue para o nº2".

MUSIC AND SOUND
- Background music: warm, upbeat Brazilian acoustic (light bossa nova / acoustic guitar + soft percussion). Make it softer and more emotional (just guitar/piano) during the sofa "problem" scene, then bring the beat back when the squats start. If you can't use a music file, generate a simple acoustic-style track with code.
- Duck the music under the voice (voice always clear, music about 10-15% under speech, louder when nobody speaks).
- Light nature ambience: birds, a rooster at the start.
- Sound effects: whoosh on every transition, a soft "pop" when each text card appears, a light "boom" on the hook text, a heartbeat-like thump on the sofa struggle, a small "ding" when "10x POR DIA" appears.

EFFECTS
- Slow zoom-in (Ken Burns) on every shot; a quick punch-in zoom on the first word of the hook.
- White flash at the very start; slide/whip transitions (0.3s) between scenes.
- Sofa scene slightly desaturated and darker (the "problem"); everything after it warm, bright and saturated.
- Warm, sunny colour grade overall. Fade out at the end.

Before rendering, list the clips you received, their lengths and how you will use each one. After rendering, show me 4-6 still frames (hook, sofa, squats, crates, ending) so I can check subtitles and cards, then give me the download link.

--- Prompt de edição (cole com os vídeos) ---
You are my video editor. I am uploading the clips for an animated Instagram/TikTok video by Rebecca, a 64-year-old AI grandma who talks about health and strength for women over 60 from her farm in Brazil. Use code (ffmpeg / Python) to edit the clips into ONE finished video file I can download. If some clips are missing, edit the ones I uploaded and tell me which scenes are missing.

FORMAT: vertical 9:16, 1080x1920, 30 fps, MP4. Crop horizontal clips to vertical, keeping Rebecca's face in frame. Trim silences and dead frames. Remove any AI watermark. Fast pace, no shot longer than about 5 seconds without a zoom or cut.

SUBTITLES (in Portuguese, exactly what is said): big bold ALL CAPS, 2-3 words at a time, white with a thick black outline, lower-middle of the screen, pop-in animation. Keywords in GREEN (#2FA84F) for positive words (FORÇA, LIBERDADE, SAÚDE, PROTEÍNA, EQUILÍBRIO, TODOS OS DIAS) and RED (#E5202B) for warnings (IDADE, QUEDA, DIETAS, MENTIRA, SEM ENERGIA).

COLD OPEN (scene 1): start mid-action with no fade-in and no logo; the first frame must already be tense. Add a low boom + short riser under the first second, show the suspense card big in the first second, then cut hard to the next scene. TEXT CARDS: the hook text at the top in a black box for the first 3 seconds; the short card texts from the script (e.g. "10x por dia") big and centered with a pop-in; at the end, at the top: "Segue para o próximo hábito".

MUSIC AND SOUND: upbeat, warm Brazilian acoustic music (light bossa nova / acoustic guitar) at about 10-15% volume, ducked under the voice. Soft nature sounds (birds, rooster at sunrise). Whoosh on transitions, a soft "pop" when each card appears.

EFFECTS: slow zoom-in on every shot, slide transitions (0.3s), warm colour grade, a quick flash on the hook, fade out at the end.

Before rendering, list the clips you received and what goes on each. After rendering, show me 4-6 still frames to check, then give me the download link.

SCRIPT:
Hook text: "3 mentiras que te contaram sobre envelhecer"
Scene 1: "Disseram-me isto aos sessenta. Era mentira."  [card: ERA MENTIRA]
Scene 2: "Três mentiras que te contaram sobre envelhecer."
Scene 3: "Mentira um: é tarde demais para ganhar força. Não é. O corpo responde em qualquer idade."  [card: ❌ "É tarde demais"]
Scene 4: "Mentira dois: tens de comer como um passarinho. Precisas de comida a sério para manter o músculo."  [card: ❌ "Come pouco"]
Scene 5: "Mentira três: descansar é ficar parada. Descansar também é dançar e rir."  [card: ❌ "Fica quieta"]
Scene 6: "Aos sessenta e quatro, nunca me senti tão forte. Segue a tua avó de IA."

Scene 5: "Most people save what's left. Old money spends what's left."
Scene 6: "Do it with your next paycheck. Comment 'ENVELOPE' if you're starting this month."  [card: COMENTA: ENVELOPE]

IMPORTANT: never put any text over a face; place cards in the middle of the screen over the body or background.

--- Prompt de edição (PT) ---
You are my video editor. I am uploading the clips for an animated Instagram/TikTok Reel for my page @simone_dynasty. Use code (Python / ffmpeg) to edit them into ONE finished MP4 I can download. If clips are missing, edit what I uploaded and tell me which scenes are missing.

FORMAT: vertical 9:16, 1080x1920, 30 fps. Crop horizontal clips to vertical, keeping faces inside the frame. Trim silences and dead frames. Remove any AI watermark. Fast pace, no shot longer than about 5 seconds without a zoom or cut.

SUBTITLES (English + Português (PT), exactly what is said): big bold ALL CAPS, 2-3 words at a time, white with a thick black outline, lower-middle of the screen (about 30% from the bottom), pop-in animation. Keywords in RED (#E5202B): RICH, OLD MONEY, FIRST, ENVELOPE, 10%, 72 HOURS, CASH, VALUE, KEEP, PRICE, PRIORITY, ASSET, LIABILITY / RICO, PRIMEIRO, ENVELOPE, 72 HORAS, GUARDAS, PREÇO, PRIORIDADE, ATIVO, PASSIVO.

COLD OPEN (scene 1): start mid-action with no fade-in and no logo; the first frame must already be tense. Add a low boom + short riser under the first second, show the suspense card big in the first second, then cut hard to the next scene. TEXT CARDS: the hook text for the first 3 seconds and the short card texts below, big and bold with a pop-in. IMPORTANT: never put any text over a face; place cards in the middle of the screen over the body or background.

MUSIC AND SOUND: soft jazz piano / classy lo-fi (or a trending sound added in the app), about 10-15% volume, ducked under the voice. If you can only generate music with code and it sounds cheap, leave music out and tell me (I'll add a trending sound in the app). Whoosh on transitions, a soft pop when each card appears.

EFFECTS: slow zoom-in on every shot, 0.3s slide transitions, a quick flash on the hook, fade out at the end.

Before rendering, list the clips and what goes on each. After rendering, show me 4-6 still frames, then give me the download link.

--- Prompt de edição ---
You are my video editor. I am uploading the clips for an animated Instagram/TikTok Reel for my page @billionaira.fenix. Use code (Python / ffmpeg) to edit them into ONE finished MP4 I can download. If clips are missing, edit what I uploaded and tell me which scenes are missing.

FORMAT: vertical 9:16, 1080x1920, 30 fps. Crop horizontal clips to vertical, keeping faces inside the frame. Trim silences and dead frames. Remove any AI watermark. Fast pace, no shot longer than about 5 seconds without a zoom or cut.

SUBTITLES (Português (PT), exactly what is said): big bold ALL CAPS, 2-3 words at a time, white with a thick black outline, lower-middle of the screen (about 30% from the bottom), pop-in animation. Keywords in RED (#E5202B): ZERO, ANÓNIMA, ROSTO, ERRO, ERROS, NICHO, CONSISTÊNCIA, NÚMEROS, ESTRATÉGIA, TODOS OS DIAS.

COLD OPEN (scene 1): start mid-action with no fade-in and no logo; the first frame must already be tense. Add a low boom + short riser under the first second, show the suspense card big in the first second, then cut hard to the next scene. TEXT CARDS: the hook text for the first 3 seconds and the short card texts below, big and bold with a pop-in. IMPORTANT: never put any text over a face; place cards in the middle of the screen over the body or background.

MUSIC AND SOUND: elegant, modern lo-fi / soft deep-house beat, about 10-15% volume, ducked under the voice. If you can only generate music with code and it sounds cheap, leave music out and tell me (I'll add a trending sound in the app). Whoosh on transitions, a soft pop when each card appears.

EFFECTS: slow zoom-in on every shot, 0.3s slide transitions, a quick flash on the hook, fade out at the end.

Before rendering, list the clips and what goes on each. After rendering, show me 4-6 still frames, then give me the download link.

Hook: "3 erros que cometi na primeira semana"
Scene 1: "Publiquei sete dias seguidos. Resultado: quase zero."  [card: 7 DIAS. QUASE ZERO.]
Scene 2: "Três erros que cometi na primeira semana da minha página."  [card: 3 ERROS]
Scene 3: "Erro um: falar de tudo. Ninguém segue uma página sem tema."  [card: ERRO 1: SEM NICHO]
Scene 4: "Erro dois: publicar uma vez e desaparecer. Consistência vale mais do que perfeição."  [card: ERRO 2: SEM CONSISTÊNCIA]

--- Prompt de edição ---
You are my video editor. I am uploading the clips for an animated Instagram/TikTok Reel for my page @caioenegocios. Use code (Python / ffmpeg) to edit them into ONE finished MP4 I can download. If clips are missing, edit what I uploaded and tell me which scenes are missing.

FORMAT: vertical 9:16, 1080x1920, 30 fps. Crop horizontal clips to vertical, keeping faces inside the frame. Trim silences and dead frames. Remove any AI watermark. Fast pace, no shot longer than about 5 seconds without a zoom or cut.

SUBTITLES (Português (BR), exactly what is said): big bold ALL CAPS, 2-3 words at a time, white with a thick black outline, lower-middle of the screen (about 30% from the bottom), pop-in animation. Keywords in RED (#E5202B): PREÇO, GRÁTIS, LUCRO, CLIENTE, TRUQUE, POUCO DINHEIRO, NEGÓCIO, ESCOLA.

COLD OPEN (scene 1): start mid-action with no fade-in and no logo; the first frame must already be tense. Add a low boom + short riser under the first second, show the suspense card big in the first second, then cut hard to the next scene. TEXT CARDS: the hook text for the first 3 seconds and the short card texts below, big and bold with a pop-in. IMPORTANT: never put any text over a face; place cards in the middle of the screen over the body or background.

MUSIC AND SOUND: upbeat, modern hip-hop / funky beat with light percussion, about 10-15% volume, ducked under the voice. If you can only generate music with code and it sounds cheap, leave music out and tell me (I'll add a trending sound in the app). Whoosh on transitions, a soft pop when each card appears.

EFFECTS: slow zoom-in on every shot, 0.3s slide transitions, a quick flash on the hook, fade out at the end.

Before rendering, list the clips and what goes on each. After rendering, show me 4-6 still frames, then give me the download link.

SCRIPT:
Hook: "3 negócios para começar com pouco dinheiro"
Scene 1: "Dá pra começar um negócio com menos do que custa esse tênis."  [card: MENOS QUE UM TÊNIS]
Scene 2: "Três negócios que dá para começar com pouco dinheiro."  [card: 3 IDEIAS]
Scene 3: "Um: doces caseiros por encomenda. Começa na sua cozinha."  [card: 1. DOCES POR ENCOMENDA]
Scene 4: "Dois: limpeza de carro em domicílio. Você vai até o cliente."  [card: 2. LIMPEZA DE CARRO]
Scene 5: "Três: cuidar das redes de pequenos comércios do seu bairro."  [card: 3. REDES SOCIAIS]
Scene 6: "Nenhum é dinheiro fácil, mas todos começam pequeno. Tem mais trinta ideias no link da bio."  [card: +30 IDEIAS NA BIO]

4 · NARRAÇÃO COLE NA FERRAMENTA DE VOZ
############################################################

--- Voz da Simone ---
The Whitmores inherited forty million dollars… and still went broke.
Old family. Old money. A mansion on the hill.
When the old man died, his grandson Charles took over.
Charles loved a party. Every weekend.
New cars. A yacht. A villa in Monaco.
Here's the thing. Forty million, invested, earned about two million a year.
Charles was spending five.
You see the problem, darling.
Every year, three million came out of the principal. Twelve years later…
Fortunes rarely die from one big mistake. They bleed… from a lifestyle.
Old money lives on what the money earns. Never on the money itself.
That's mistake number one. Follow for number two.

The maths checks out: losing $3M a year from $40M, with less income each year as the principal shrinks, runs out in about 12 years.)*

-

 Editing notes
**Music:** soft piano or string quartet. When the party starts (scenes 4–5), switch to upbeat jazz. At scene 9 (the crash), cut the music to silence for 1 second, then bring back a sad piano.
**Sound effects:** champagne pop (sc. 4), cash register "cha-ching" (sc. 6), paper rustle (sc. 7), teacup clink (sc. 10), gavel hit when **$0** appears (sc. 9).
**Number cards:** a white card with a big bold number. Green for income, red for spending and for $0. Animate the crossed-out line drawing across $40M.
**Captions:** white text in a black rounded box, in the bottom third. Keep it clear of Instagram's buttons.

 Post caption
They had $40 million. They didn't have a rule. 🫖
Mistake #1 of 5 that kill fortunes. Which one is next? 👇
*Fictional story for educational purposes.*
#oldmoney #moneymindset #generationalwealth #financialliteracy #wealthmindset

--- Prompt de edição ---
You are my video editor. I am uploading the clips for an animated Instagram Reel called "The Whitmores inherited $40M... and still went broke" (Mistake #1 of 5 that kill fortunes). The narrator is Simone, an elegant old-money "rich auntie". Use code (ffmpeg / Python) to edit the clips into ONE finished video file I can download. If I could not upload every clip, edit the ones I did upload and tell me which scenes are missing.

CLIP ORDER (file names roughly match the scene numbers):
1. Simone in her library (hook)
2. The mansion on the hill
3. Charles with his feet on the desk
4. The champagne party
5. Charles on the yacht
6. The accountant
7. Charles buried in bills
8. Simone sipping tea
9. The ruined mansion, 12 years later
10. Simone setting her teacup down
11. Simone raising her teacup in a toast (ending)

FORMAT
- Vertical 9:16, 1080x1920, 30 fps, MP4 (H.264 + AAC), total length about 35-50 seconds.
- If a clip is horizontal, crop it to vertical and keep the speaking character's face inside the frame (do not cut off the face).
- Trim silence and dead frames at the start and end of every clip. Keep the pace fast; no shot longer than about 5 seconds without a zoom or cut.
- Cover or crop out any AI watermark (for example the small sparkle logo in the bottom-right corner).

SUBTITLES
- Burn in big, bold subtitles, 2-3 words at a time, ALL CAPS, white text with a thick black outline, placed in the lower-middle of the screen (above the Instagram buttons, about 30% from the bottom).
- Each word group pops in with a quick scale-up animation.
- Keywords in RED (#E5202B): WHITMORES, FORTY MILLION, DOLLARS, BROKE, OLD MONEY, MANSION, PARTY, YACHT, TWO MILLION, FIVE, PROBLEM, TWELVE YEARS, BLEED, LIFESTYLE, MISTAKE, EARNS, NEVER.
- Match the subtitles to what is actually said in each clip. The script is:
  Simone: "The Whitmores inherited forty million dollars... and still went broke."
  Simone: "Old family. Old money. A mansion on the hill."
  Simone: "When the old man died, his grandson Charles took over." Charles: "Grandpa's gone. Now the fun begins!"
  Simone: "Charles loved a party. Every weekend." Charles: "Champagne for everyone! It's on the Whitmores!"
  Simone: "New cars. A yacht. A villa in Monaco." Charles: "Life is too short for small boats!"
  Accountant: "Mr. Whitmore, your money earns two million a year." Charles: "Wonderful. Then I'll spend five."
  Charles: "Sign here, sign there... money is for spending!"
  Simone: "You see the problem, darling."
  Simone: "Every year, three million came out of the principal. Twelve years later..." Charles: "Where did it all go?"
  Simone: "Fortunes rarely die from one big mistake. They bleed... from a lifestyle."
  Simone: "Old money lives on what the money earns. Never on the money itself. That's mistake number one. Follow for number two."

TEXT CARDS (big, bold, centered, pop-in animation)
- 0-6s, at the top in a black box: "MISTAKE #1 THAT KILLS FORTUNES" (the "#1" in red).
- On the mansion: "$40,000,000".
- On the accountant scene: "+$2M A YEAR" ($2M in green).
- On the bills scene: "-$5M A YEAR" ($5M in red).
- On the ruined mansion: "12 YEARS LATER..." then "$40,000,000" with a strikethrough and a big red "$0" under it.
- Last 3 seconds, at the top in a black box: "MISTAKE #1 OF 5" and smaller "FOLLOW FOR #2".

MUSIC AND SOUND
- Background music: soft, elegant piano (classy, old-money feel) for the opening and mansion, upbeat light jazz for the party / yacht / spending scenes, a tense ticking-clock feel for the money cards, then slow sad piano from the ruined mansion to the end. If you cannot use a music file, generate a simple piano track with code.
- Duck the music under every voice (voice always clear, music about 10-15% volume).
- Sound effects: whoosh on every transition, champagne pop on the party, cash-register "ding" on +$2M, low boom on -$5M and on $0, soft teacup clink when Simone sets the cup down.

EFFECTS
- Slow zoom-in (Ken Burns) on every shot.
- Quick white flash at the very start and on the cut to the ending.
- Slide or whip transitions between scenes (0.3s).
- Ruined-mansion scene: darker and less colorful (desaturated) to feel sad.
- Fade out at the very end.

Before rendering, list the clips you received with their length and what you will put on each. After rendering, show me 4-6 still frames so I can check the subtitles and cards, then give me the download link.
